				add_country_modifier = { name = mastered_country_market duration = -1 }

					remove_province_modifier = mastering_region_mark

	core_regions_calculation_ENG = {
		potential = {
			tag = COM
			exists = ENG
		}
		allow = {
			ENG = {
				any_owned_province = {
					is_colonial = no
					NOT = { has_province_modifier = core_reg }
					state_scope = { any_owned_province = {
						any_neighbor_province = {
							has_province_modifier = core_reg
							owned_by = ENG
						}
					}}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			ENG = {
				any_owned = {
					limit = { state_scope = {
						is_colonial = no
						any_owned_province = {
							any_neighbor_province = {
								has_province_modifier = core_reg
								owned_by = ENG
							}
						}
					}}
					state_scope = { any_owned = {
						add_province_modifier = { name = core_reg duration = -1 }
					}}
				}
			}
		}
	        ai_will_do = { factor = 1 }
	}

			tag = COM
			SUR = {
				any_owned_province = {
					has_province_modifier = calculation_of_core_regions
				}
				NOT = {
					any_owned_province = {
						is_colonial = no
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = core_reg }
						state_scope = { any_owned_province = {
							any_neighbor_province = {
								has_province_modifier = core_reg
								owned_by = SUR
							}
						}}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		allow = {}
		effect = {
			SUR = {
				any_owned = {
					limit = { has_province_modifier = calculation_of_core_regions }
					remove_province_modifier = calculation_of_core_regions
				}
			}
		}
	        ai_will_do = { factor = 1 }
	}

	market_is_mastered_ai_decision = {
                alert = no
		potential = {
			ai = yes
			is_independant = no
			overlord = { OR = {
				nationalvalue = nv_commercial_capitalism
				nationalvalue = nv_industrial_capitalism
				nationalvalue = nv_imperialism
			}}
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = mastered_country_market
				has_country_flag = not_mastered_yet_vassal
				any_owned_province = { has_building = selector }
			}
		}
		allow = {}
		effect = {
			remove_country_modifier = mastering_country_market
			add_country_modifier = { name = mastered_country_market duration = -1 }
			any_owned = {
				remove_province_modifier = mastering_region_mark
				remove_province_modifier = mastering_the_region
				add_province_modifier = { name = mastered_region duration = -1 }
			}
		}
	        ai_will_do = { factor = 1 }
	}

	release_ENG_comp = {
		alert = no

			tag = COM

		potential = {
			tag = COM
			any_owned_province = { is_core = SWI }
		}
		allow = {}
		effect = {
			any_owned = {
				limit = {
					is_core = SWI
					owned_by = THIS
				}
				secede_province = SWI
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 1 }
	}

		potential = {

		}

			}
